### Page 1

門間 一夫の 
経済深読み

成長戦略より21 世紀ニッポンの「 ア
ポロ計画」を 
みずほ総合研究所  
エグゼクティブエコノミスト  門間一夫 202 6 年 10 月 27 日

心情的にはノーサレンダー路線 
先月の論考では、①度重なる成長戦略にもかかわらず日本の潜在成長率は低下したこと、②成長戦略
には政策手段の特定が困難という基本問題があるこ と 、③25 年英知を尽くしても結果が出なかった事実
は厳粛に受け止めるべきこと、などを述べた 1。 
それを踏まえ、本稿ではあるべき方向性を考えてみたい。選択肢は、(a)25 年で諦めず50 年でも100
年でもやる、(b)成長目標を達成しやすい水準に下げる、(c)政府のミッションは成長戦略とは別にある
と考える、の 3 つである。選択肢(b)が無難に見えるが、もともと成長戦略は「現状の成長率ではだめ
だ」という認識に基づくものである。現状を明確に上回る成長率を目指さない限り、成長戦略としては
自己矛盾である。 
すると残るのは (a)ノーサレンダー路線、(c)ミッション再考、 のいずれかである。このうちノーサレ
ンダー路線、つまり「成長を諦めない」という不屈の姿勢には人々の共感が得られやすい。しかし、不
屈の志を掲げても、手段の裏付けを欠くという現実は変わらない。手段の裏付けを欠くなら「目標」で
はなく「夢」である。もちろん、夢を語ることが悪いわけではないし、政治家は夢を語るべきという考
えもあるだろう。その夢が偶然にでもかなえば歴史に残る偉業であり、夢なら実現せずとも落胆の度合
いは小さい。ノーサレンダー路線はアップサイドだけでダウンサイドはない、と考えてよいだろうか。 
成長戦略のダウンサイド 
成長戦略にダウンサイドはない、やってみて損はない、と い う前提は実は正しくない。成長戦略は「た
だ」でできるわけではないからだ。例えば、成長戦略には多かれ少なかれネット財政支出が伴う。国債
をどれだけ発行できるのか議論の余地はあるが、いずれにせよ有限である。 
一方、成長戦略は総花的になりやすい。成長を実現する手段がはっきりしない以上、ある政策案を「成
長とは無関係」ときっぱり排除するのも難しいからである。経済界の要望や政治家の信念に基づく様々
な政策案が、成長に少しでも関係しそうなら幅広く正当化される。各種施策の費用対効果について一定

1 門間一夫の経済深読み「成長戦略の本質的な限界」 （2026年 9 月25 日 ）。

### Page 2

2

の分析や議論は行われている。しかし、行われることとそれが有効に機能することは別である。費用対
効果の分析が有効に機能し、各種施策の経済成長へのインパクトが正確に測れていたとしたら、25 年に
わたり成長目標の大幅未達という結果にはならなかったはずである。 
一般に、政策目標の実現に向けてリソースが投入され、目標が実現できなかった場合、そのリソース
投入は失敗だったことになる。チャレンジ自体は無駄ではなかったと言える場合もあるが、過去 25 年
だめだった目標にもう一度挑戦してみるという話なら、たぶん次もだめだろうと事前に予想できる。金
も人もふんだんにあるなら別だが、財政状況が厳しく、人材の不足も叫ばれる国で、高確率で失敗する
ミッションへのリソース投入は賢明な選択と言えるのか。 
有限のリソースが無駄に使われれば、他の課題のために使えるリソースが減る。ここで言うリソース
は財政資金だけではない。実現できない目的のために、多忙な官僚の労力が割かれ、政治家のポリティ
カル ・ キャピタルも消耗すれば、やればできたはずの他の課題への対応まで中途半端に終わってしまう。
成長戦略には他の課題を犠牲にするという「機会費用」が伴うのであり、それが「ただではない」とい
うことのより正確な意味である。「成長を諦めないノーサレンダー路線でいく」という選択は、「50 年で
も 100 年でもその機会費用を払い続けることを覚悟する」という選択でもある。 
何のための経済成長か 
ここで話を一段階前に戻してみよう。そもそもなぜ、経済成長を高めるべきと考えられているのだろ
うか。もちろん、低成長より高成長の方がいいに決まっているので、経済成長を簡単に高められるので
あれば、そんな疑問を持つ必要はない。しかし、経済成長を高めようとすることで機会費用を払い続け
ることになる確率が高いなら、そうまでしてわれわれが最終的に望んでいるものは何なのかという問い
に、きちんと向き合う必要がある。 
経済成長の必要性を訴える主張は、「〇〇の解決には経済成長が不可欠」「低成長のままなら〇〇のよ
うな不都合が起きる」というパターンにおおむね当てはまるように思う。つまり、現在直面している課
題、あるいは将来直面しそうな課題に対応するには、より高い経済成長が必要という認識が成長戦略の
基礎にあると考えられる。念頭に置かれている課題の中身は、社会保障や財政の持続性、行政・医療・
介護サービスの維持、国際的な存在感の回復、生活水準の向上など、論者により様々である。パイを増
やせるなら改善できる課題は多いので、経済成長を高めるべきという主張は万人受けし、政治家には格
好の政策アジェンダになる。 
しかし、「何らかの課題を経済成長で解決する」という考え方は 、「その課題に直接向き合うより経済
成長を高める方が簡単だ」という前提がないと成立しない。その前提が間違っていれば、むしろ経済成
長に頼るがゆえに肝心の課題がいつまでも解決しないことになる。重要な課題であればあるほど、経済
成長と切り離して解決する努力を早く始めるべきではないか。 
例えば、社会保障や財政の持続性が課題なのであれば、給付と負担のバランスや、恵まれている人と
そうでない人の公平感を改善することで、問題の解決を目指す方が良い。人々の生活水準を高めたいと
いう課題を経済成長と切り離すのは少し厄介だが、国民全員が等しく困っているわけではなく、問題の

### Page 3

3

本質は経済格差にある。分配政策により、国民生活の満足感や社会の安定性を全体として改善すること
はできる。 
国際社会における日本の存在感を高めることは、経済成長ではそもそも無理であろう。かつて日本が
世界第2 位のGDP をキープし、経済大国として存在感を示した時代もあった。しかし、あれは冷戦構造
のもとでの日米関係、戦後の高度成長とその余熱、新興国が発展初期段階にあったことなどによる歴史
の偶然であった。GDP の対世界シェアが3～4％程度まで落ち、今後さらに人口が減り、資源・エネルギ
ーに乏しく、特殊な言語を使う島国が、今から経済成長を多少高められたとしても、そのことで示せる
国際パワーは高が知れている。小さくても存在感がある国をどう作るかが、正しいアジェンダ設定だと
思われる。 
21 世紀ニッポンの「アポロ計画」は？ 
日本が課題だらけなのは確かだが、25 年間どうやっても達成できなかった経済成長に、その解決をこ
れ以上頼るのが正解とは思いにくい。逆に、優先課題の解決に国が本気でリソースを投入するなら、そ
こに民間も収益機会を見出し、様々な連鎖反応を通じて経済に好影響が及ぶ可能性も出てくる。 
経済学者マリアナ・マッツカートは著書「ミッション・エコノミー」において、大きな変革は市場メ
カニズムだけでは生み出せず、国家が野心的だが実行可能な課題を設定し、民間の力を引き出す仕組み
を築くことが重要と説く 2。マッツカートは代表例として、1960 年代の米国アポロ計画を挙げる。1969
年 7 月 20 日、 ニール・アームストロングとバズ・オルドリンが月面に降り立ったことで知られる、あの
アポロ計画である。 
日本の成長戦略も「野心的な課題」を掲げたものではあるが、複雑すぎるマクロ経済そのものを課題
の対象にすると、先月の論考で詳しく述べたように、目的と手段の関係性が特定できない。原因と結果
の関係がよくわからない仕事は、責任の所在があいまいで互いに人任せになり、結果にコミットするこ
との効果が得られない。それに対し、「月に人を送る」などのより具体的なミッションの場合、そのため
には何が必要なのかの因果関係を分析・認識しやすい分、結果にコミットすれば人々の力が正しい方向
へ向かう。 
アポロ計画は、半導体、コンピューター、通信、材料工学などの技術を飛躍的に発展させ、その後の
米国の産業基盤を築いたと言われている。また、計画推進の国家機関であった米航空宇宙局 （NASA）は 、
多くの科学技術人材を育成・輩出した。彼らはその後、金融イノベーションなどでも活躍し、NASA 出身
なので「ロケット・サイエンティスト」と呼ばれたことはよく知られている。 
ここで重要なのは、半導体やコンピューターなどの産業育成自体を目的として、政府が補助金で支援
したという話ではないことである。アポロ計画という具体的なミッションがまず存在し、それへの貢献
に適正な対価で報いる仕組みがイノベーターたちを突き動かし、様々な技術や知見が開発、蓄積された
のである。後世に残った産業基盤や人材は、「月に人を送る」というミッションの副産物であった。

2 Mazzucato, Mariana(2021) Mission Economy: A Moonshot Guide to Changing Capitalism, Allen Lane-Penguin 
（関美和・鈴木絵里子訳『ミッション・エコノミー』News Picks、2021 年 ）。

### Page 4

4

一般論として、何らかの課題を解決すれば、経済に必ず大きなプラスの効果が見込める、とまでは言
えない。しかし、上位目標が「課題の解決」なのだから、それが達成された時点でミッションは成功で
あり、投入されたリソースは「有効に使われた」ことになる。そのうえで、マクロ経済にもプラスの波
及効果があればなお良いが、仮にそれがなくても課題が解決された分、以前より望ましい社会になって
いる。 
最大の難関は、優先課題を特定しそれに取り組む社会的モメンタムを生み出すことであり、それには
既存の発想に捉われない強力なビジョンとリーダーシップが要る。アポロ計画にも当初「無謀で財政資
金の無駄使い」という声があった。マッツカートは先ほどの著書の中で、仮に現代風の費用対効果分析
を適用していたら、アポロ計画は採用されず、人類が月面に立つことはなかったと言っている。 
アポロ計画に相当する案件は、どの時代、どの国にも自明なものとしてあるわけではない。 「月に人を
送る」というミッションは、当時の米ソ冷戦状況において、ソ連に宇宙開発で打ち勝つことが米国の死
活問題、という歴史特殊的な認識のもとで生まれた。それでも、よく考えてみれば今の日本にも、人口
の減少 ・ 高齢化や地政学環境の激変など、 人類史上にもまれな歴史特殊的な条件のもとで、 「死活問題」
になりつつある課題はいくつかあるように思う。例えば、エネルギーや食料の安定供給、生活インフラ ・
医療・介護体制の維持・改善などは、次世代の日本人にとってまさに「死活問題」なのではないか。 
限られたリソースの優先課題への配分が、市場メカニズムで実現できないなら、国が強力に関与しな
ければならない。民間には不得意な国民的課題の解決こそ、国の本来の役割であり、そのためのリソー
スを国債発行でまかないきれないなら、国民に追加負担を求めてもいい。 
言うまでもなくこれは困難な作業である。だから優先課題は何か、そのために国民負担を求めるべき
かなどを深く議論せず、経済成長で様々な問題をまとめて解決できることにしておく方が、実はずっと
楽なのである。しかし、それが問題の先送りにすぎないことは繰り返し述べたとおりである。今も成長
戦略が重要だと言い、政府にそれを求めたがるわれわれの意識は、日本が直面する真の課題やその優先
順位を考えるのは面倒だ、 という思考停止から生まれているのかもしれない。21 世紀ニッポンの「アポ
ロ計画」は何なのか、国民的な議論の活性化が必要ではないだろうか。

• 9月綜合PMI升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速 ，新訂單及就業亦
明顯成長 ，顯示消費 、AI投資與國防支出支撐景氣動能 。不過，需求快速擴張也加劇供應鏈
瓶頸，企業投入價格升至 2022年10月以來高點，燃料、運輸及原物料成本上升 ，顯示景氣韌
性與通膨壓力同步升溫，增加Fed政策維持緊縮的壓力。
美國
期中選舉
• 目前民調顯示 ，民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ，這將有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期 ，進
而帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑 ，對長天期美債形成支撐 。此外既有資料顯示 ，選後一年美股
平均報酬率達12.4%，主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到經濟成長、企業獲利及貨
幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。

越南
• 越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數 。估計此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流
入越南。資產管理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約 25億美元。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風 ，除了能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經濟成長 ，今年以來越南成為
美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成為中國商品流入的中轉國 ，美對越的301條款
調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。

美國景氣強勢擴張，通膨與利率壓力再升
• 企業活動創逾五年新高 ，製造業與服務業同步加速擴張 ：S&P
Global美國9月綜合PMI初值升至58.4，創2021年7月以來新高，
且連續四個月加速成長；服務業 PMI升至58.7（59個月高點）、
製造業PMI升至57.0（52個月高點），產出亦同步加速 ，加上
訂單與就業成長皆達近年最快 ，顯示內需、AI投資及國防支出
共同支撐企業活動。S&P Global依PMI調查推估經濟年成長約
5%，Q3則上看4%，反映短期景氣動能明顯增強。
• 供給受限推高成本 ，通膨壓力再升溫： 需求快速擴張同時推高
供應鏈與產能壓力 ，企業未完成訂單升至 2022年5月以來高點 ，
供應商交貨延遲亦明顯惡化 ，反映產能不足 ，投入價格指數升

至2022年10月以來新高 ，企業主要反映燃料 、運輸、原物料
及薪資成本上升 ，顯示供給受限與能源成本可能持續向終端價
格傳導，增加通膨黏著風險並提高Fed維持緊縮政策的壓力。
• 後市觀察：數據公布後，10年期美債殖利率一度升至約 5.12%，
創2007年以來新高 ，市場對10月再升息的押注亦升溫 ，短線
需關注供應鏈壓力是否緩解 ，以及後續ISM、就業與物價數據
能否確認通膨再升溫趨勢 。投資策略建議關注利率敏感度較低
的高品質成長股或防禦型資產 ，可適度布局受惠強勁內需與 AI
／國防支出的美股大型科技與工業類股 ，並留意升息環境下金
融股的利差受益機會 ，整體建議維持分散配置並嚴控利率風險 。
資料來源：S&P Global PMI, Bureau of Economic Analysis via S&P Global Market
Intelligence， 2026.9.24

能源成本升高推升通膨，利率維持高檔壓力升溫
• 能源與運輸成本上升，增加通膨黏著風險：中東衝突與
俄羅斯煉油設施遭攻擊 ，使全球柴油供應持續偏緊 ，燃
料價格及運輸成本同步上升；由於柴油廣泛應用於運輸 、
農業與製造業，價格上漲將透過物流及生產成本向下游
傳導。目前全球柴油庫存偏低 、煉油產能受限，若供應
中斷延續，可能使能源價格維持高檔並延長通膨壓力。
• 公債與企業債供給增加 ，長端利率壓力升高：美國財政
赤字及政府融資需求偏高 ，加上大型科技公司持續為 AI
資料中心等基礎建設籌資 ，債券供給增加使市場承接壓
力升高；在通膨預期未完全穩定的情況下 ，投資人對長
天期債券要求較高的期限溢酬 ，進一步推升長端殖利率 ，
同時AI相關企業債近期亦出現利差擴大 ，反映市場對大

量發債及資本支出回收期的關注升高。
• 資產配置面臨成長與通膨雙重拉扯： 能源價格、企業融
資成本及長端殖利率若持續處於高檔 ，將提高企業資金
成本並壓縮高估值資產的評價空間；同時 ，利率與能源
價格波動也可能擴大股 、債市震盪。因此資產配置建議
降低長天期債券對利率變化的敏感度 ，提高對通膨與利
率波動的防禦能力 ，並以現金流穩定、具備定價能力之
優質標的為核心。
資料來源：Federal Reserve Bank of St. Louis via FRED ， 2026.9.8

資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.9.9

美國期中選舉-目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數
• 目前民調顯示民主黨可能取得兩院或眾議院多數 ：最新民調顯示，民主黨
於11月美國期中選舉，取得國會多數席次的機率已領先共和黨，根據市場
情境分析，期中選舉不影響既有法案執行 ，但將左右未來兩年的財政預算 、
稅制改革 、政府支出及國會監督權限 。情境(1)若共和黨續掌國會 ，減稅
政策延長、產業回流、關稅及移民限制等議程有望加速推進 ，財政擴張力
道可能進一步增強 。情境(2)分裂國會 ，兩黨制衡將使重大法案推動難度
提高，新增減稅與大型財政刺激方案較難落地 。情境(3)民主黨全面掌控
國會，則可能提高對政府政策的監督力度 ，政策方向偏向提高稅負與監管 ，
壓抑企業獲利 ，使政策環境趨向平衡。

美國期中選舉-對於股債市的影響
• 對股市的影響：美國銀行分析1900至2025年共31次美國期中選舉，資料發現標普500在期中選舉前12個月平均報酬約為2.9%，
低於所有年度平均 8.9%。因為市場在選舉前往往受到政策不確定性 、政黨競爭及投資人觀望情緒影響 ，波動相對較大 。然而
此現象不代表選舉必然導致市場疲弱，因為當時的經濟環境、通膨、利率及企業獲利等因素同樣扮演重要角色。期中選舉後12
個月的股市表現通常較佳 。歷史數據顯示，選後一年美股平均報酬率達12.4%。主因為政治不確定性降低，市場焦點重新回到
經濟成長、企業獲利及貨幣政策等基本面因素，進而帶動投資信心提升。
• 對債市的影響：若民主黨取得國會主導權，市場普遍預期財政支出擴張幅度將較受節制 ，有助於縮減未來財政赤字預期，進而

帶動10年期美債殖利率下滑，對長天期美債形成支撐，若共和黨持續全面掌控國會，市場可能預期減稅及財政擴張政策延續，
進一步推升財政赤字預估，帶動公債殖利率走高，對長天期美債形成壓力，不過市場已部分反映相關預期，實際衝擊幅度有限。
美國期中選舉前後對於美股的影響

AI建設熱潮吞噬自由現金流
• 資本支出創新高：雲端服務巨頭(包括亞馬遜、Alphabet、微軟、Meta、甲骨文以及SpaceX等)正在全力擴充AI算力與資料
中心，2026年大型雲端服務巨頭的資本支出合計預估將接近 9,600億美元，而2025至2031年的累計AI資本支出更可能逼近
9兆美元。
• 自由現金流出現赤字： 高額的投資加上股東回報(股息與庫藏股買回)，使許多巨頭的自由現金流(FCF)轉為負值(如Alphabet、
亞馬遜、微軟、Meta及甲骨文等)，預計到2027年，部分企業將更加依賴外部融資，而非單靠內部現金生成。
• 債券發行量大增： 為了填補資金缺口 ，科技業債券發行規模急遽上升 ，2026年迄今，各幣別的投資級科技債券發行量已突
破4,000億美元，創下歷史新高。

獲利韌性與金庫深度：強勁EBITDA成為信用安全網
• 上兆美元的流動性護城河： 儘管債務增加，但科技十二巨頭合計持有近 1兆美元的等值現金，整體科技板塊流動性充裕 ，短
期內無流動性危機。
• EBITDA強勁成長消化債務：市場預期覆蓋的12家最大科技企業在2026與2027年的EBITDA合計將成長約35%，強勁的獲利
能力顯著降低槓桿比率的上升壓力，確保信評機構維護穩定的信用評級。
• 資本配置策略分化：(1)兼顧股東回報：蘋果、微軟、Meta與Alphabet在投入AI同時，仍維持大規模的庫藏股買回與股息發
放。(2)全數投入資本支出：亞馬遜將絕大部分現金導向資本支出(2026年資本支出預計達2,200億美元居首)，且不發放股息。
• 小結與投資建議：科技巨頭為維持AI技術與基礎設施領先優勢，短期內，負自由現金流將成為常態。然而，這並非財務危機

訊號，而是企業利用超強獲利能力 (高EBITDA)與龐大的現金儲備進行戰略性擴張 ，科技板塊的信用風險目前處於可控且健
康的狀態，投資建議聚焦AI變現能力與EBITDA成長，優先佈局具有強大獲利護城河且能將算力轉化為實質營收的龍頭企業 。

越南股市正式納入富時羅素新興市場指數
• 預估引導60億美元資金流入越南： 富時羅素估計，此次納入最高可引導 60億美元的資金重新流入越南 。受此提振，資產管
理公司Vanguard也計畫在未來幾年內，將對越南的投資額提升至約25億美元。整個指數過渡將分為四個階段推行至2027年：
今年9月率先納入10%，明年3月追加20%，最後於明年6月與9月分別完成剩餘各35%的分配。市場分析指出，隨著明年3月
配額加大，市場關注度與對本地投資人的影響將更為顯著。
• 越南股市短期仍面臨逆風： 儘管官方早前誓言今年 GDP成長目標達10%，但中東衝突以來能源通膨壓力居高不下恐拖累經
濟成長。此外，越南與美國的貿易協商仍在進行中，由於今年以來越南成為美國最大貿易逆差國，加上美方企圖阻止越南成

為中國商品流入的中轉國，美對越的301條款調查為越南出口額帶來更多不確定性。
• 觀點：儘管短線面臨不確定性 ，但越南股市同時具備多項利多題材 ，包含企業獲利成長、公共投資加速、FDI回流及資本市
場改革等。中長期結構仍偏正向，可以衛星配置的角度布局，或是以定期定額的方式參與長期成長潛力。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026.09。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

### Page 3

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
3
3Q Result Miss, Expecting Recovery in 4Q26
Delta’s 3Q earnings and GM were below expectation due to component shortages
and delays in data center deployment. GM declined 1.6ppts QoQ to 34.1%,
primarily reflecting inventory provisions equivalent to 1.7% of revenue, driven by
supply chain mismatches, customer delivery postponements, and product
specification adjustments. Nevertheless, OPM remained resilient at 16.6%, broadly

flat QoQ and YoY, supported by operating leverage.
Management indicated that AI-related businesses across its four divisions now
account for approximately 50% of consolidated revenue, highlighting Delta’s
increasing exposure to AI infrastructure investment. Management remains
confident in Delta’s competitive positioning, particularly its integrated capabilities
spanning power conversion, power distribution, thermal management, and
infrastructure solutions. Although the industry continues to face component

shortages, especially for certain power semiconductor devices, Delta believes its
supply chain management capabilities compare favorably to competitors. The
company also plans to establish a major manufacturing facility in Penang,
Malaysia, leveraging the region’s established infrastructure supply chain and
engineering talent.
Figure 1. Delta - 3Q26 earnings review
© 2026 Citigroup Inc. No redistribution without Citigroup’s written permission.
Source: Company Reports, Citi Research Estimates, Bloomberg consensus

HVDC Adoption and SOFC Commercialization Create
Additional Long- T erm Growth Opportunities
Delta expects the transition toward high-voltage DC power architecture to
accelerate as AI rack power density continues to increase. The company began
shipping ±400V HVDC solutions in 3Q26, while NVIDIA is promoting 800VDC
architectures for next-generation AI infrastructure. Management believes HVDC
adoption will eventually become widespread given its potential to improve power

conversion efficiency and reduce distribution losses, although standardization,
installation requirements, and shortages of qualified electrical workers could
constrain the near-term rollout. Beyond power conversion, Delta is developing
solid oxide fuel cell (SOFC) solutions to address growing demand for distributed
power generation. The company plans to begin commercial production in 2027,
with an initial manufacturing line providing approximately 100MW of annual

capacity, primarily supporting customer qualification and pilot installations. Initial

customers include domestic utility and industrial users, alongside several overseas
customers. Management emphasized that SOFC shipments will remain limited
initially as the company prioritizes product reliability and qualification.
4Q26 Recovery Expected as Inventory Normalizes, with
Positive Growth Outlook for 2027
Management expects revenue to increase sequentially in 4Q26 as delayed
customer deliveries resume and inventory begins to normalize. Gross margin

should improve from 3Q26 levels, although management remained cautious given
potential year-end inventory adjustments. The 3Q26 inventory provisions largely
reflected customer-specific products rather than structural obsolescence, and
some provisions could potentially be reversed following successful deliveries.
Nevertheless, management acknowledged that inventory buffers will likely remain
structurally higher than historical levels to accommodate volatile AI infrastructure

deployment schedules. Raw material costs have increased approximately 2% year-
to-date, prompting ongoing pricing negotiations with customers, with limited
recovery expected in 4Q26 and a more meaningful impact in 2027. Operating
expenses will continue increasing in absolute terms to support R&D and capacity
expansion. Management remains positive on 2027 revenue growth, although it did
not provide quantitative guidance. Separately, management expects no near-term

shipment disruption from the ongoing Vicor intellectual property litigation.
We lower earnings projection on power shortages, labor constraints, financing
conditions, and regulatory uncertainties could create quarterly shipment volatility,
but we believe its multiyear AI infrastructure growth cycle intact. We maintain Buy
with TP NT$1,715 (based on lower PER of 35x of 2027 EPS, from 40x previously)

Bull/Bear: Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
Prepared for Kevin Lu

### Page 7

Delta Electronics (2308.TW)
29 October 2026 Citi Research
7
Delta Electronics
Company description
Delta was established in 1975 and is the world's leading computer switching
power supply manufacturer. Its business is divided into: 1) Power Electronics
(59% of 2022 revenue), including Switching Power Supply, Components,
Display and EV Solution; 2) Infrastructure (27% of revenue), including Wind
power, Telecom power, UPS, EV Infrastructure, Networking; 3) Automation

(14% of revenue), including Industrial Automation (IA), Building Automation;
and 4) Others ( <1% of revenue).
Investment strategy
We rate Delta Electronics shares as Buy due to its strong execution and the
ability to expand margin through favorable product mix shift to AI power and
thermal solutions. We believe EV business slowdown is in the price, and we
expect margins to improve driven by AI solution spec upgrades and expanding
business scale. Delta is now positioning itself as an infrastructurer enabler

rather than a pure component supplier, which deepens its key position in the
AI supply chain.
Valuation
Our target price for Delta Electronics of NT$1,715 is based on 35x 2027E EPS
estimate. Our target multiple is set at the higher end of its trading range since
2023, which we believe is warranted by Delta’s strong earnings growth and
better product mix prospects. We also believe the company’s leading industry
position, long product development cycle, and strong engagement with

customers should translate into high orders/earnings visibility.
Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price
are: 1) lower contribution from non-PC businesses; 2) products in the new
businesses (LED lighting, electric vehicle, and Voice) growing more slowly
than expected; and 3) PC power supply losing market share.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ASE disclosed a strong set of quarterly results and guidance 
on 29 October, both beating market expectations, thanks to the strong AI 
scaling which is just getting stronger to benefit ASE’s leading-edge 
advanced packaging (LEAP) business. We raise our forecasts and 12M TP 
and reaffirm our Outperform (2) call on the stock; accumulate into any 
technical pullback.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. ASE reported 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD27bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 25%/18% 
thanks to higher semiconductor assembly & test (SAT, or IC ATM by 
company definition) revenue which came in at TWD151bn (USD4.7bn), 7% 
above our estimate, for higher gross margin (GM) of 30.5%, 1.9pp above 
ours and topping ASE’s structural target of 24-30%. The strength was 
amplified further by lower non-operating losses, resulting in the strong 
bottom-line beat (table on p.2). Guidance was another beat thanks to the 
strong SAT business where ASE expects revenue to grow 11-13% QoQ for 
GM to top its record of 30.5% during the COVID upturn. We raise our 2026-
28E EPS by 14-24% assuming its SAT GM to reach 33% through 2027.

Strong AI gets stronger. Discussions centred on ASE’s LEAP business, 
where management further hiked revenue target this year beyond USD4bn 
(USD1.6bn in 2025), after 3 hikes YTD, thanks to strong demand for its on-
substrate (oS) packaging and initial ramp of its full-process solutions. With 
addition of new co-packaged optics (CPO) solutions, ASE expects the 
LEAP revenue to double YoY in 2027. As LEAP is margin-accretive, ASE 
expects the SAT GM to continue improving beyond 4Q26, making the 
ceiling of its structural GM target now as the floor to kick off 2027 for a 
strong start. Given management’s emphasis on “friendly pricing”, we expect 
likely continued cost passthrough across the supply chain to add to GM 
upside. Working hard to narrow the supply/demand gap, ASE further raised 
this year’s capex to USD11.5bn (from USD10.5bn), reinforcing our view 
that AI is scaling strongly to accelerate the chip industry growth.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for ASE to TWD710 (previous: TWD550), based on our revised ROE-
adjusted PBR target of 5.8x (previous: 5.2x) 2027E BVPS due to a higher 
ROE trajectory. Our revised TP translates into 24x 4-quarter forward PER, 
which we see as justifying the rich PBR target given its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 25%. We reiterate our Outperform (2) call with key downside risks 
of any “silicon inflation” and geopolitical overhang leading to AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-10% above the consensus as we 
believe the street has yet to factor in its stronger LEAP business.

29 October 2026

Entering an even stronger earnings cycle (III)

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Strong AI scaling is getting even stronger to benefit LEAP 
➢ Reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD710

Company profile 
Established in April 2018, ASE Industrial Holding Co (HoldCo) is a holding company which privatized 
ASE and SPIL fully on 30 April 2018 through equity and cash swap, respectively, and listed on the same 
day with senior management assigned mainly by ASE. HoldCo has become the strong No.1 OSAT 
maker in the world, offering a comprehensive product portfolio to its customers from wirebonding, flip-
chip & bumping, system-in-package (SiP) to broad-based electronics manufacturing services (EMS).

### Page 5

5

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
ASE’s Board of Directors consists of nine members with diverse professional backgrounds.
Three of the directors are independent, representing 33 percent of the board. The Chairman also
serves as the Chief Executive Officer, resulting in unified leadership and centralized decision-
making. To maintain effective governance and oversight under this structure, ASE relies on its
independent directors and established board processes. The board currently includes one
female director, and the company continues to pursue long-term goals related to board diversity
and balanced representation. ASE has an Audit Committee, Compensation Committee, Risk
Management Committee, and Chief Government Officer to conduct effective supervision.
Capital management 3
In the past 5 years, ASE paid over 50% or nearly 50% of its earnings in dividends. However, the
dividend trend was volatile in dollar and payout ratio terms. We forecast its dividend payout ratio
to average over 50% in the next 2 years.
Related party &
transaction 2
ASE undertakes related-party transactions with its subsidiaries on sales and purchases. The
total related-party transaction sales/purchase amounts were less than 10% of its total
sales/purchases, and the transaction terms with related parties were normal compared with
those with other customers/suppliers. Thus, we do not think related-party transactions have a
meaningful impact on overall operations.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
The supply of raw materials has the most direct impact on ASE's operations and manufacturing.
To lower overall supply-chain risks, ASE has expanded the scope of sustainability risk
management to non-tier-1 suppliers. ASE currently has over 600 non-tier-1 suppliers.
ASE requires its suppliers to comply with its Supplier Code of Conduct, which aims to ensure
that suppliers conduct business in a manner that meets labour, health & safety, environment,
business ethics & management, and various corporate compliance standards.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
ASE has adopted 3 water-use strategies of reduce, reuse and recycle. Through its water
management measures, it reduced water consumption density (water consumption/revenue) by
43% in 2024 vs. that of 2015. ASE is committed to increasing water reuse at its facilities in order
to improve water efficiency.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
ASE is committed to improving waste recycling and reuse through the 5 key elements of a
circular economy of: direct recycling, reuse, off-site regeneration, renewable materials, and
supply and rental. The recycling rate of general waste was 90% in 2024 and its hazardous waste
density declined by 53% in 2024 vs. that of 2015.
E GHG emissions 1
ASE is committed to achieving net zero emissions by advancing low carbon energy adoption,
developing smart grid technologies, and strengthening high tech energy management systems.
As of 2024, 19% of total manufacturing energy consumption comes from renewable sources,
88% of ASE facilities use renewable energy, and 10 sites have achieved RE100 certification.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

• Hon Hai's partnership with NVIDIA is extending across GPU, CPUs, LPU,

conventional cables, liquid-cooling hoses and fans, simplifying assembly,

installation and high-density connector insertion. We believe deeper 
design-for-manufacturing and design-for-automation capabilities should

and as AI systems become increasingly complex. We also believe this will be 
the core competence for Hon Hai to secure next-generation rack-scale 
systems, where manufacturing complexity and system-level validation 
increasingly differentiate suppliers.

more than 10 million components. By completing rack integration, cabling, 
liquid-cooling connections and functional testing before shipment, the 
solution aims to reduce onsite deployment from approximately six months 
to as little as 14 days. NVIDIA expects its partner ecosystem to deliver

TECO on modular data centers that integrate IT systems, power distribution and cooling within 40-foot containers,
with more than 80% of modular assembly completed at the factory. We believe this model could meaningfully
expand Hon Hai’s revenue opportunity beyond rack assembly into fully integrated infrastructure, while accelerating
time to first revenue-generating AI tokens.
• Hon Hai highlighted its expanding role in next-generation AI networking, including NVIDIA’s Spectrum-6 SN6800

CPO switch, delivering 409.6Tbps aggregate bandwidth through 512 ports at 800Gbps. Hon Hai showcased
capabilities spanning CPO optical components, ELSFP modules, high-speed connectivity and complete switch-tray
integration, positioning itself to capture additional networking content as optical interconnect adoption accelerates.
Next, we expect the scale-up CPO with NVL576 to be announced with VRU next year; Hon Hai will remain the sole
supplier, and in our view, its first-mover advantage will be supported for further revenue and earnings upside.

• Other than VR200 system, Hon Hai also demonstrated Nvidia's LPUs, integrated into its Vera Rubin ecosystem
through LPX inference racks containing 256 LPUs. Unlike GPUs optimized for parallel computation, LPUs
accelerate latency-sensitive token generation, particularly feed-forward and mixture-of-experts execution,
complementing Rubin GPUs handling prefill and attention workloads. We believe the heterogeneous GPU-LPU
computing could expand Hon Hai's addressable market in AI infrastructure integration.

• Beyond AI Data Centers: Hon Hai highlighted progress in EV, autonomous mobility, robotics and smart healthcare.  In
EVs, it introduced the affordable Model A multi-purpose vehicle, targeting Japan and Southeast Asia. Its CDMS
strategy focuses on shortening development cycles and improving cost competitiveness. In robotics, Hon Hai
demonstrated physical AI, humanoid robots and digital-twin-enabled manufacturing automation, supported by its
collaboration with NVIDIA and ABB Robotics.  The company also showcased emerging capabilities in SiC

semiconductors, low-Earth-orbit satellites and FoxBrain AI models. We believe these initiatives reinforce Hon Hai's
longer-term transformation into a diversified technology manufacturing platform.

Hon Hai Precision
Valuation
Our target price of NT$400 is based on 18x average 2026E-27E EPS, which is at the higher end of the historical trading range.
We believe this multiple is justified by the company’s enhanced sales growth and profit margin outlook, as well as new
endeavors into AI server, EV, digital health and robotics. Hon Hai has been able to generate positive earnings streams, and thus

we believe a P/E valuation methodology is appropriate.
Risks
Downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected iPhone demand;
2) intensified peer competition; 3) weaker-than-expected execution on new business progress; and 4) higher-than-expected
impact of COVID-19 or other macro factors.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and

Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:
Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in

reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is
moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 6

Globalwafers Co (6488.TWO)
04 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Globalwafers Co
Company description
GWC is the world's third-largest silicon wafer manufacturer. The company
offers a one-stop shop for customers with its wide range of product portfolio
and global presence. GWC has 16 manufacturing sites in 10 countries spread
across Asia, Europe, and the Americas. Its product offering include annealed,
polished, non-polished, diffused, FZ, EPI, SOI wafers ranging from 4” to 12” in

diameter.
Investment strategy
We rate GWC as Buy as we expect its sales and earnings to recover in 2027.
We believe silicon wafer sector has hit the bottom in 2Q26, evidenced by UTR
improvements across 6” to 12” wafers. Although GM would likely face short-
term headwinds in 2H26 due to rising depreciation burden, a fire incident and
higher energy costs, we believe favorable pricing trend, full UTR and the
reception of US government’s subsidies would help offset the negative

impact, especially in 2027.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$920 is based on 28x 2H27-1H28E EPS, in line with
+1stdv of 1-year fwd PE in the past 5 years. We use this PE multiple to reflect a
gradually improving demand outlook for the overall semiconductor industry
from 2H26 onward, especially a more meaningful recovery in 2027 and likely
shortage. We believe the upcoming megatrend demand from AI, HPC, auto,
and Io T and supply tightness could justify GWC's valuations during recovery

backdrop from 1Q27 onwards.
Risks
Key downside risks that could cause the GWC shares to trade below our
target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected pull-in demand; 2)
undisciplined capacity expansion by other industry participants; 3) wafer
price decline driven by macro factors; and 4) late reception of the subsidies
from the US government.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: We provide a 3Q26 results preview on Sunon, which plans to 
release its 3Q26 results on 6 November. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call as we 
expect the 4Q26 and 2027 outlook for its telecom, servers, industrial and 
auto segments to remain intact with both gross and operating margin set to 
expand. We now forecast a net profit CAGR of 28.2% over 2026-28E from 
21.2% previously (see our flash, 28 August).

What’s the impact: 3Q26 results preview. Sunon’s 3Q26 revenue of 
TWD6,143m (+13.4% QoQ and +24.5% YoY), a record-high level, 
accounted for 97.2% and 102.2%, respectively, of our previous 
(TWD6,319m, +16.6% QoQ and +28.1% YoY) and the Bloomberg 
consensus estimates for 3Q26 revenue. We forecast its 3Q26 gross margin 
to be 32.8% (vs. 31.3% in 2Q26 and 31.3% in 3Q25), slightly higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of 31.9%. We forecast an operating 
profit of TWD1,115m (+33.3% QoQ and +45.0% YoY), 3.0% higher than 
the Bloomberg consensus estimate of TWD1,082m and an operating 
margin of 18.1% (vs. 15.4% in 2Q26 and 15.6% in 3Q25). Overall, we 
forecast a net profit of TWD901m (+30.5% QoQ, +29.7% YoY), 1.9% 
higher than the consensus estimate of TWD884m. We forecast 4Q26 
revenue to grow 7.5% QoQ, and 1Q27 revenue to grow 1.4% QoQ 
considering its ASIC and GPU projects.

Rising liquid cooling revenue and EC fan exposure in 2027E underpin 
its gross margin expansion. We expect Sunon’s cold plate products (ie, 
enterprise and GPU clients), pump and CDU to be the key revenue drivers 
with certification also underway for CSP clients. We expect revenue from 
EC fans to reach 10% in 2027E for the industrial segment. We revise up 
our 2026-28E EPS by 1-13%, mainly on higher revenue, gross margin and 
operating margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD265 (from TWD236), still based on a target PER of 18x vs. its past-
5-year PER range of 6-25x, on the 2027E EPS, given solid earnings 
visibility from intact demand in the server, telecom, industrial and auto 
segments. We believe its dividend yield of 5.6-9.2% over 2026-28E will 
offer downside support for its share price. Downside risks: worse-than-
expected gross-margin expansion and AI server development progress.

How we differ: We are one of only two overseas brokers covering the 
stock. Our 2026-28E EPS are 4-12% above the Bloomberg consensus, 
likely due to our more aggressive revenue, gross margin and operating 
margin estimates.

Company profile 
Founded in 1980 and listed on Taiwan’s main board in 2000, Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry 
(Sunon) is one of the top-4 fan and motor providers in the world, along with Delta Electronics, Nidec and 
ebm-papst. Its major clients are Apple, Dell, Lenovo, Bosch, Siemens and Tesla. Its applications include 
IT & OE, telecoms & servers, industrial & medical equipment, home applications, distribution, autos and 
LED lighting.

Sunonwealth Electric Machine Industry (2421 TT): 3 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 8 May 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
The CEO and chairperson of Sunonwealth are the same individual; Sunonwealth's board is led
by the Hung family. We see no issues with the company’s board. We view the chairman leading
the R&D team as a sign of the company’s continuing focus on developing new products to
achieve a long-term competitive advantage.
Capital management 2 Sunonwealth's dividend payout ratio was 83-88% in 2015-18, but it decreased to 60-69% during2022-25 due to its capacity expansion plans.
Related party &
transaction 2 Revenue generated from related-party transactions was less than 1% of total revenue during2023-25.
E Materials sourcing &efficiency 1
Sunonwealth’s sourcing of raw materials has been relatively stable (including IC, copper, plastic,
aluminium and steel), and we also note that Sunonwealth abides by RoHS regulations and
relevant international standards (ISO14001, IECQ QC080000, ISO9001) for its material sourcing
activities. With the ongoing ESG trend, its clients may move towards a more energy-efficient
production cycle, which will likely benefit Sunonwealth’s energy-efficient products.
E Product design &lifecycle management 2
Sunonwealth has improved its product designs to stronger but lighter form factors. Besides, we
have observed more green projects utilising Sunonwealth's capabilities. Thus, we believe
Sunonwealth has strong product design and lifecycle management capabilities to address the
ongoing changing business environment.
S Product quality &safety 2 Sunonwealth has its own Quality Strategy Centre to evaluate quality issues. With internationalstandards in place, we believe Sunonwealth has taken relevant measures to address productquality and safety issues.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

股價相對大盤走勢

晶豪科 (3006 TT)  Trading Buy

報價收斂，後續成長動能受限，建議 Trading Buy 。  
投資建議 ：AI 伺服器對 HBM 多層晶圓堆疊的強勁需求大幅吸
納三大原廠先進 DRAM 產能 ， 引發傳統記憶體供給端嚴重缺口 ，
缺貨潮由 DDR5 與 DDR4 蔓延至 DDR3 、 DDR2 及 NOR/NAND 
Flash 。同業大廠如華邦電 (2344 TT) 基於晶圓廠經濟效益考量，
產能優先配置予大容量 DDR4 產品，對 DDR2 與 DDR3 採取停
供或減供措施，大幅限縮市場流通供給。目前晶豪科 DDR2 與
DDR3 合計佔 DRAM 營收比重超過 50% ，在同業減供退出的趨
勢下，晶豪科已成為市場核心主要供應商，市占地位結構性擴
張。預估晶豪科 2027 年稅後 EPS 127.72 元。雖然記憶體價格
在 2027 年仍可以維持高檔，但目前報價漲幅開始收斂，產能供
給受限，成本持續上揚，後續成長動能受限，因此對晶豪科投
資建議為 Trading Buy 。  
3Q26 稅後 EPS 36.09 元： 受惠國際大廠把產能轉至 HBM、DDR5
等高階產品時，成熟型 DRAM、DDR2/DDR3 等利基型記憶體供
給減少，帶動報價上揚，晶豪科 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元，
QoQ+58.83% ，毛利率增加 8.75 個百分點至 77.44% ，3Q26 稅後
淨利 107.59 億元，QoQ+82.12% ，稅後 EPS 36.09 元。1Q26~3Q26
營收 433.63 億元，年增 3.439 倍，毛利率 69.62% ，較 2025 年
同期增 60.08 個百分點，歸屬母公司淨利 187.3 億元，稅後 EPS 
64.54 元。  
預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 39.41 元： 展望 4Q26 營運表現，儘管整體
出貨數量受限於上游晶圓代工與封測端產能配額難見大幅擴
增，但由於 4Q26 產品平均銷售單價 (ASP) 延續上漲趨勢，漲幅
約 10~20% 。但晶圓代工從 07/2026 漲約 45% ，封測約漲 20% ，
將會逐步反映在 4Q26 銷貨成本，經營團隊預期 4Q26 毛利率將
穩健維持在 3Q26 之歷史高檔區間，來自產品售價調升幅度足
以抵銷成本增幅。預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 39.41 元。預估 2026 年
稅後 EPS 102.25 元。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 3 日

2 群益投顧 
  晶豪科 ESG ：  
企業永續與治理架構  
• 企業永續委員會：於 10/2020 成立，負責評估永續經營的風險與機會、制
訂永續發展願景與策略。  
• 核心推動構面：涵蓋強化公司治理、夥伴關係、環境永續、友善職場及社
會公益。  
• 永續報告書：定期依循國際標準 (如  GRI 、 TCFD 、 SASB 等 )編製與揭露
年度永續報告書 ， 可至晶豪科技 ESG 報告書或耀智永續報告書資料庫  查閱
與下載。  
環境與氣候行動  
• 減碳目標：積極因應全球氣候變遷，設定具體減碳目標，其減碳表現經評
估朝向升溫  1.5°C 以下的目標邁進 (實測減碳溫度約  1.506°C) 。  
• 永續金融連結：曾與金融機構 (如台新銀行、北富銀等 )合作推動永續績效
聯貸案 (30 億元聯貸案 )，將  ESG 指標與授信條件結合，落實綠色金融與企
業社會責任。  
社會責任與人才培育  
• 社會公益與教育：設立「晶豪科技教育基金會」，長期投入教育與社會公
益相關推廣。  
• 人才與實習計畫：積極與青年世代接軌，辦理暑期研發與  ESG 實習生計
畫，深化校園人才交流與職場體驗。  
記憶體價格調漲，晶豪科 3Q26 營收 221.50 億元， QoQ+58.83% ，稅後淨利
107.59 億元， QoQ+82.12% ，稅後 EPS 36.09 元：  
晶豪科為國內利基型記憶體  IC 設計公司，產品涵蓋利基型 DRAM 產品組
合，涵蓋從傳統 SDRAM 到主流的 DDR 、 DDR2 、 DDR3 及 DDR4 。廣泛應
用於消費性電子、工業控制、網通設備、 PC 週邊，公司主要是 4Gb 以下為
主。在 Flash 方面，晶豪科有 SLC NAND Flash 以及編碼型快閃記憶體 (NOR 
Flash) 。因應中低階智慧手機、系統級封裝 (SiP) 及固態硬碟 (SSD) 需求快速
成長，公司以 MCP/eMCP/eMMC 為主力產品。類比和數位的產品
(Audio/Power/Motor Driver/Sensor) 佔公司營收比重低。各產品佔比， DRAM
佔營收比重最高，約 60~65% ， Flash 和其他約佔 30~33%(MCP 等也有含
DRAM) ，非記憶體佔營收比重 5%。  
受惠國際大廠把產能轉至 HBM 、 DDR5 等高階產品時，成熟型 DRAM 、
DDR2/DDR3 等利基型記憶體供給減少，帶動報價上揚，晶豪科 3Q26 營收
221.50 億元， QoQ+58.83% ，毛利率增加 8.75 個百分點至 77.44% ， 3Q26 稅
後淨利 107.59 億元， QoQ+82.12% ，稅後 EPS 36.09 元。  
累計 1Q26~3Q26 營收 433.63 億元，年增 3.439 倍，毛利率 69.62% ，較 2025
年同期增 60.08 個百分點，歸屬母公司淨利 187.3 億元，稅後 EPS 64.54 元。

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 3 日

3 群益投顧 
3Q26 底晶豪科的存貨為 90.76 億元，庫存水準較 2Q26 的 79.56 億元，
QoQ+14.08% ，要反映晶圓廠與封測調價造成的成本墊高，而非量的顯著增
加。  
合作晶圓廠現階段增產幅度相對受限，預估 4Q26 營收 243.02 億元，
QoQ+9.72% ，稅後淨利 117.49 億元， QoQ+9.20% ，稅後 EPS 39.41 元：  
晶圓代工端產能配置方面，晶豪科 DRAM 主要晶圓代工廠為力積電 (6770 
TT) ，也是力積電記憶體最大的代工客戶。主要代工夥伴力積電因先前將部
分廠房出售予 Micron(MU US) ，現有 12 吋晶圓廠中 P1 與 P2 廠原生產邏輯
代工，在 3Q26 調整至生產記憶體代工，但整體而言，記憶體代工產能釋出
受限，爭取晶圓代工與後段封測產能 (Wafer & Backend) 成為晶豪科當前最
關鍵的營運課題。  
力積電於 07/2026 全面啟動新一波代工報價調漲，其中針對 DRAM 投片價
格大幅調升約 45% 。考量自晶圓投入、前段製造至出貨之生產週期 (Cycle 
time) 約需 5~6 個月，晶豪科配合客戶訂單展開拉貨後，該批高價晶圓成本
將於 4Q26 正式反映於銷貨成本 (COGS) 之中。主要封測代工夥伴南茂 (8150 
TT) 亦自 07/2026 起調漲 20% 之封測費用，受限於約 6 個月之封測與成品週
轉週期，亦遞延至 11/2026 正式認列於營業成本。  
然而，由於市場端面臨華邦電等同業對成熟製程 DDR2 與 DDR3 減供或停
供的結構性缺口，終端訂單滿足率目前僅達 60% 至 70%，晶豪科具備極為強
勁的賣方定價話語權。在 4Q26 產品 ASP 持續走高，且漲幅 10~20% 的帶動
下 ， 晶豪科有能力將晶圓與封測端增加的成本全數轉嫁予客戶端 ， 支撐 4Q26
毛利率穩健維持在 3Q26 之 77.44% 歷史高檔區間。預估晶豪科 4Q26 營收
243.02 億元， QoQ+9.72% ，稅後淨利 117.49 億元， QoQ+9.20% ，稅後 EPS 
39.41 元。  
預估晶豪科 2027 年營收 898.37 億元， YoY+32.77% ，稅後獲利 380.72 億
元， YoY+24.91% ，稅後 EPS 127.72 元：  
晶豪科的產品組合，記憶體佔營收比重最高，約 95%。記憶體當中， DRAM
佔 65% 左右，以 KGD 為主。其他則為 NAND 、 NOR 以及其他
MCP/eMCP/eMMC 等。 DRAM 當中， DDR2 和 DDR3 佔目前 DRAM 出貨量
50% 。  
市場競爭格局方面， AI 伺服器對 HBM 多層晶圓堆疊的強勁需求大幅吸納
三大原廠先進 DRAM 產能，引發傳統記憶體供給端嚴重缺口，缺貨潮由
DDR5 與 DDR4 蔓延至 DDR3 、 DDR2 及 NOR/NAND Flash 。同業大廠如華
邦電基於晶圓廠經濟效益考量 ， 產能優先配置予大容量 DDR4 產品 ， 對DDR2
與 DDR3 採取停供或減供措施，其中華邦電之 DDR2 僅保留供應特定策略
夥伴，大幅限縮市場流通供給。目前晶豪科 DDR2 與 DDR3 合計營收比重
超過 50%，在同業減供退出的趨勢下，晶豪科已成為市場核心主要供應商，
市占地位結構性擴張。  
終端需求方面， AI PC、筆記型電腦與智慧型手機單機搭載記憶體容量提升，
加上實體 AI( 如人形機器人與自駕載具 )逐步落地，帶動非 HBM 記憶體需求
持穩增長。消費性電子端雖因記憶體 ASP 漲幅較大出現部分觀望情緒，惟
晶豪科高階與商務應用拉貨依然暢旺，客戶訂單滿足率受限於產能瓶頸，整

體營運動能無虞。展望 2027 年營運，管理層指出全球傳統記憶體產能供給
增長極為有限，供需失衡格局短期難以扭轉，預期市場供不應求狀態將延續
至 2027 年全年，支撐產品報價結構性維持堅挺， 2027 年整體營運維持樂觀

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아 쉬 웠 습 니 다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추 정 치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계 
금융투자상품의 비중 96.4% 3.6% 0.0% 100.0%

Document 231: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
여전히 가격 상승 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 95.8조원, 영업이익 75.1조원으로 예상한다. 
이전 전망 대비 감소한 수준이다. 원/달러 환율 영향이다. 영업이익률은 78.4%로 전 
분기 대비 2.1%p 상승할 전망이다. DRAM 매출액은 73.1조원, 영업이익 
59.2조원으로 추정한다. DRAM ASP는 이전 전망 대비 상향 조정했다. 일부 제품 
가격 인상을 반영한 영향이다. Bit Growth는 이전 전망 수준을 유지했다. ASP 
상향이 수익성 개선에 미친 영향은 크지 않을 전망이다. NAND 매출액은 22.4조원, 
영업이익은 15.9조원으로 예상한다. 
Global Top 수준 주주환원 규모/자사주 살 게 더 있다 
SK하이닉스는 2026년 8월 19일 주주환원에 대한 새로운 정책을 제시했다. FCF에서 
CAPEX를 제외한 금액의 50% 이상을 주주환원에 사용한다는게 핵심이다. 2026년 
기준으로 최소 90조원 수준일 것으로 추정한다. 자사주 중심으로 주주환원을 계획 
중이고 이미 집행 중인 40조원 이외에 4분기 중에 추가로 자사주 매입이 진행될 
것으로 예상한다. 배당도 일부 집행이 되겠지만 자사주가 중심이 될 전망이다. 
흔들리지 말아야 할 이유는 많다 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 모델의 성장으로 메모리에 
대한 수요가 지속적으로 높아지고 있고, 이에 비해 공급 증가는 제한적인 상황이 
상당기간 지속될 것으로 전망하기 때문이다. 이로 인해 현재 수준의 DRAM, NAND 
가격에 변동성은 낮고, 높은 수익성이 유지되면서 안정적 현금흐름 창출이 가능할 
것으로 전망한다. 현재 주가는 지나치게 저평가되어 있는 국면에 있다고 판단한다. 
목표주가는 12개월 예상 EPS 446,092원에 PER 9 배를 적용한 4,000,000원을 
유지한다.

동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

Document 291: AI抛售潮中买家消失！高盛交易台电话被“打爆”却无人接盘，对冲基金、散户齐撤

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI抛售潮中买家消失！高盛交易台电话被“打爆”却无人接盘，对冲基金、散户齐撤.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛交易员称，周三是该⾏股票交易台全年最繁忙交易⽇之⼀，周四完全相反，投资者纷
纷询问市场还可能跌多深，却⼏乎看不到买盘进场；部分对冲基⾦开始卖出周三刚刚买⼊
的股票。瑞银数据显⽰，周四抛压主要来⾃对冲基⾦。以摩根⼤通数据估算，散⼾净卖出
约 13 亿美元，单⽇规模创⼀年新⾼。 “ 恐慌指数 ”VIX 盘中飙升⾄⼋个⽉⾼位。投资者抢购保
护性期权，主要股指看跌期权权利⾦为通常两倍。
摘要：
美东时间 11 ⽉ 5 ⽇周四，美股科技股遭遇猛烈抛售之际，⾼盛集团的股票交易台电话⼏乎被 “ 打
爆 ” ，投资者纷纷打来询问市场还可能跌多深，但真正愿意逢低买⼊的资⾦却寥寥⽆⼏。
⾼盛交易员 John Flood 在发给客⼾的市场评论中形容，周四的交易环境与前⼀⽇截然相反：周
三是该⾏股票交易台全年最繁忙的交易⽇之⼀，⽽周四则 “ 完全相反 ” ，交易员接到⼤量询问市场
情况的电话，却⼏乎看不到买盘进场。
这番描述折射出 AI 交易突然降温后，市场情绪从追涨迅速转向避险。纳斯达克综合指数周四收跌
5.52% ，费城半导体指数暴跌超过 12% ，科技股整体市值蒸发约 2.5 万亿美元。与此同时，波动

率飙升、散⼾集中卖出、对冲基⾦削减仓位，进⼀步放⼤了市场跌势。
⾼盛交易员眼中的市场反转：周三积极买⼊，周四急于撤退
Flood 表⽰，周三市场上涨后，部分此前在美国中期选举前⼤幅降低⻛险敞⼝的投资者重新⼊
场，买回了⼀些股票。但周四市场突然逆转，部分对冲基⾦⼜开始卖出前⼀⽇刚刚买⼊的股
票，尤其是⼤型科技股和超⼤市值科技公司。
他指出，⻓期投资机构再度陷⼊观望，市场尚未出现愿意出⼿托底的买家。与此同时，⾯向散
⼾客⼾的券商也反映，卖盘连续第⼆天明显多于买盘。
交易活跃度同样出现异常。 Flood 称，截⾄周四盘中，标普 500 指数 ETF SPY 成交量较过去 20 个
交易⽇均值⾼出约 160% ，追踪纳斯达克 100 指数的 QQQ 成交量⾼出约 100% ，半导体 ETF SMH
成交量⾼出约 150% ， DRAM 相关股票成交量则⾼出约 75% 。
成交量激增反映出投资者正在密集调整仓位，但并不意味着所有交易都是净卖出。结合交易员
所描述的买盘缺位，市场更明显的信号是：在股价快速下跌时，原本可能⼊场承接的资⾦选择

了等待。
机构抛压集中释放，散⼾单⽇净卖出规模创近⼀年新⾼
⾼盛交易台观察到的买盘退缩，也得到了其他机构交易数据的印证。
据路透社报道，瑞银交易员数据显⽰，周四该⾏机构客⼾的主动交易流向明显偏向卖出，抛压
主要来⾃对冲基⾦，科技股尤其是存储芯⽚和半导体板块遭遇集中抛售。瑞银交易员将新兴云
服务商和半导体股票的⾛势形容为明显的 “ 投降式抛售 ” 。
⾼盛交易员也指出，周四对冲基⾦正在卖出前⼀⽇重新买⼊的部分⼤型科技股。市场不仅出现
板块内部轮动，更有投资者直接削减整体⻛险敞⼝。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

散⼾投资者同样加⼊撤退⾏列。 Vanda Research 的数据显⽰，周四散⼾投资者单只股票净卖出
规模有望创下疫情以来最⼤单⽇纪录。摩根⼤通截⾄美东时间上午 11 时 30 分的数据则显⽰，若
将 ETF 计⼊，散⼾净卖出规模达到约 13 亿美元，为过去⼀年最⾼。
更值得注意的是，散⼾通常会在市场下跌时买⼊股票 “ 抄底 ” ，但这⼀次，个股市场的逢低买盘明
显减弱。 Vanda Research 策略师指出，传统的下跌⾏情中散⼾往往会买⼊回调，但周四这种买
盘实际上已经从个股中消失。
VIX 盘中飙升⾄⼋个⽉⾼位，投资者抢购保护性期权
股票抛售之外，投资者对冲市场⻛险的需求也迅速升温。
衡量市场波动的 “ 恐慌指数 ”—— 芝加哥期权交易所波动率指数（ VIX ）周四盘中⼀度上涨 11.75
点，升⾄ 28.36 ，为⼋个⽉⾼位，创 2025 年 4 ⽉美国对等关税冲击以来最⼤单⽇盘中涨幅；收盘
报 28.18 。
期权市场的变化同样显⽰，投资者正在为进⼀步下跌购买保护。路透社援引期权分析机构

Mercury Alerts 的数据称，周四主要股指产品的看跌期权权利⾦约为通常⽔平的两倍，反映出市
场对下⾏保护的需求显著增加。
互动券商⾸席策略师 Steve Sosnick 表⽰，市场情绪在短短⼀天内发⽣了明显变化：周三投资者
还认为没有必要购买保护，周四却突然意识到需要对冲⻛险。
此外，杠杆和反向 ETF 以及部分量化策略通常会在临近收盘时根据市场波动调整仓位，这可能进
⼀步放⼤尾盘涨跌。野村证券盘中估算，杠杆 ETF 周四收盘前可能需要卖出约 470 亿美元股票，
这将是该机构⾃ 2006 年开始追踪相关数据以来第⼆⼤规模的此类卖盘，且卖出压⼒主要集中在
科技和 AI 相关基⾦。
这意味着，部分卖盘并⾮完全来⾃投资者主动改变⻓期判断，也可能是市场下跌触发了策略性
减仓，进⽽形成进⼀步的下跌压⼒。
数据中⼼建设与 AI 投资回报遭质疑，抛售从芯⽚蔓延⾄产业链
本轮抛售的直接背景，是 AI 相关股票此前累积了⼤量资⾦和较⾼市场预期，⽽美国中期选举结果
⼜令数据中⼼建设前景蒙上政策阴影。

⽬采取更严格的审批、能源和环保要求，从⽽拖慢美国 AI 基础设施建设。半导体、存储芯⽚、新
兴云服务商以及数据中⼼电⼒供应商因此受到冲击。
⾼盛交易员 Flood 还提出了⼀个更具体的担忧：到 2028 年，市场可能⾯临数据中⼼⼟地、电⼒和
建筑容量不⾜的问题。如果数据中⼼基础设施建设速度跟不上芯⽚交付，市场就可能从担忧芯
⽚供应不⾜，转向担忧芯⽚过剩、缺少⾜够的数据中⼼承接。
这也解释了为何周四的抛售并不局限于芯⽚制造商。数据中⼼建设承包商、电⼒供应商、新兴
云服务商等板块也遭遇明显卖压。投资者开始重新审视 AI 产业链的扩张节奏，以及巨额算⼒投资
能否按预期转化为实际收⼊和利润。
与此同时，近期⾼通和 Arm 的业绩展望未能满⾜市场预期，也进⼀步削弱了投资者对半导体板块
的信⼼。 AI 需求⻓期增⻓的逻辑并未因此消失，但市场对增⻓兑现速度、资本开⽀回报以及产业
链各环节盈利能⼒的要求正在提⾼。

### Page 3

⻛险提⽰及免责条款

中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

Document 309: Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms

Source PDF: all report pdf/Blue Wave_ Democrats sweep the US midterms.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-11-05
Pages: 10
Status: ok

### Page 1

Blue Wave: Democrats 
sweep the US midterms  
Summary 
Event: The Democrats gained control of the House of 
Representatives and the Senate at the US midterm elections 
that took place this week.  
Our View: With the US government now no longer under 
unified control of the Republicans, gridlock has returned to 
Washington. We believe investors should not overreact to 
these results and should stay invested. 
In equities, investors should  continue to diversify their 
equity exposure into more defensive sectors.  As we 
transition to mid -cycle for the equity markets , we expect 
stocks to continue to deliver positive returns in the 8 -10% 
range per annum, a downshift from the torrid pace of gains in 
the earlier part of the cycle.  
In bonds, income remains attractive given recent spread 
widening. We favour non -hyperscaler investment grade 
corporate bonds at the three to seven -year part of the 
curve. We continue to expect the US dollar to remain range-
bound in the near  term, as we expect two more rate hikes 
from the Fed, but as this mini rate hike cycle ends in the 
second quarter of 2027, the USD index (DXY) is likely to 
depreciate towards 98.5 as markets start to anticipate a Fed 
rate cut in the second half of next year.  
Oil is likely to rebound in the short -term on heightened 
geopolitical risk, but as supply chains adapt to the current 
situation and Congress exercises oversight on defense 
spending, WTI prices are likely to drift towards our 12-month 
target of USD 80 per barrel.

Democrats made solid gains across the Senate, House 
of Representatives and State Governors 
Results reported as at 7:45PM Singapore Time

Source: The Wall Street Journal, Standard Chartered

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 5 November 2026

Major implications of Democrat control of 
Congress   
1) Fiscal expansionary policies may slow. Democrats are 
now in position to block any new tax cuts or spending plans 
from the Trump administration. At the same time, any fiscal 
policies proposed by Democrats may also face Trump’s veto. 
Hence the fiscal trajectory of the US will likely remain stuck  
over the next two years. As we get into the second half of 
2027, the US debt ceiling will once again loom, and gridlock 
could potentially bring back government shutdowns. 
However, as they have always done in the past, compromise 
will ultimately prevail, in our opinion.  
2) Trump remains in control of foreign policy.  With the 
midterms now out of the way, could Trump decide to re-start 
combat operations with Iran? We believe there is a risk that 
US and Israel – now that both countries’ have concluded 
important elections – will once again be tempted to restart 
combat operations to force an end to the conflict. However, 
the duration of any renewed hostilities may be limited by 
Congress next year as the Democrats will likely withhold 
funding for further military operations. In the near -term, oil 
prices may experience renewed volatility before stabilising at 
a lower level next year. The introduction of new tariffs on 
trading partners may prove harder with Democrat oversight. 
3) AI data centre moratoriums could grow, though model 
development regulatory changes would be harder to 
pass. While the headlines have focused on the House and 
Senate results, one important development has also 
occurred at the gubernatorial level. Notably, Democrats have 
won the governor races in Texas and Georgia, two big data 
centre states. As data centre build out permits are largely 
decided at the local state level, this will have important 
ramifications for build out plans next year.  However, any 
Democratic proposals to regulate AI model developments 
would need to be signed off by President Trump, who 
continues to wield veto power on any such proposals.   
Asset class strategy 
Equities: Remain invested and continue to diversify .  
While investors initially overreacted, President Trump’s 
relatively conciliatory response has provided some welcome 
relief, helping markets recover strongly from their pre-market 
lows. Hence, we recommend investors avoid 
overreacting, remain invested and continue to diversify 
their equity exposure across sectors and regions.  
A few themes stand out from the early pre-market reaction to 
the mid-term elections. On the positive side, defensive stocks 
continue to fare well, while themes favoured by Democrats 
are also outperforming. Clean energy is a notable 
beneficiary, with solar s tocks among the biggest winners

Policy uncertainty and a change in the innovation cycle 
is consistent with our view of a mid-cycle transition, 
where equity returns moderate but remain positive

today. Affordability-related themes are also rallying, including 
companies that could benefit from potential tariff rollbacks. 
Medicaid-related stocks are also moving higher on

On the negative side, semiconductor weakness continues

Our AI capex estimates remain conservative relative to

After downgrading semiconductors last month, we have

While we believe the mid -term election outcome is not a 
complete surprise to markets, particularly given pre -election 
polling and the recent weakness in both the S&P 500 and

investors had become, with the VIX initially surging more than

As we have highlighted in our recent notes on the transition 
to mid-cycle, this phase is typically characterised by greater 
political headwinds . T he mid -term election  outcome 
reinforces the trend. The experience of 2015 -2017, 
highlighted in the second table, offers a useful historical 
parallel. Despite the knee-jerk reaction, investors should not 
overreact based on one day’s moves. While we had trimmed 
equities to a core allocation last week, we continue to expect 
equities to deliver mid-to-high single-digit returns per annum 
during the mid -cycle, even amid political headwinds, 
supported by steady underlying earnings growth in both 2027 
and 2028. 
So, what should investors do to navigate the next few months 
before the newly elected representatives are sworn in and 
take control of the House and Senate?  As we have been 
highlighting in recent notes, equity markets should still deliver 
positive returns, albeit at a more moderate pace. Hence, we 
recommend staying invested but diversification remains key  
to navigating the next phase of the cycle.

the two structural pillars of US exceptionalism that have 
anchored dollar demand in 2026. Reduced fiscal impulse 
compresses the growth and yield differential that has kept the 
US dollar well-bid all year. Additionally, potential Democratic 
restrictions on AI and data centre investment could weigh on 
US equity outperformance, which is likely to act as a 
secondary dollar-negative channel.

DXY headwind from a Democratic sweep, the magnitude will

Fixed Income: Still attractive all -in yields ; prefer

In the past few weeks, the US Treasury yield curve has

the view the ultra-long end downshift in yields is overdone

positions now. We continue to believe the 3 to 7-year part of

less viable, and  would be cautious on some of this sector’s 
lower quality names. Meanwhile, less AI capex dependent 
issuers continue to offer solid credit profiles amidst the still 
strong economy. High yield corporate spreads have widened 
in recent weeks but dispersion wi thin the complex is wide, 
with BBs and Bs credit spreads staying relatively resilient, 
while CCCs have widened to distressed levels. For income -
focused investors, we would recommend staying within the 
crossover range of between BBB-BB.

think the Mideast situation should de -escalate given 
Democratic oversight of the defense budget, and the likely re-
configuration of oil supply chains. On the margin, we are also 
closely monitoring regulatory developments in the US shale

Document 319: Capital Market Outlook (2026-10-13)

Report source: Merrill Lynch (CMO)
Date: 2026-10-13

### Page 1

CHIEF INVESTMENT OFFICE
Capital Market Outlook

### Page 2

2 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
Consumers Feel One Way, but Spend Another
Kirsten Cabacungan, Vice President and Senior Investment Strategist
Americans have spent much of the past several years telling surveys they are cautious
about the economy. Yet they continue to spend as though they are not. The University
of Michigan’s Consumer Sentiment Index remains near multidecade lows, while the
Conference Board’s Index of Consumer Confidence has also been in a yearslong downtrend.

These surveys reflect a list of persistent concerns from elevated food and gas prices to
geopolitical uncertainty and labor market anxiety.
Even so, consumer spending has remained remarkably resilient. The final estimate of Q2
real consumer spending, as measured by PCE, was revised up to an annualized rate of
3.8%, well above the 2.3% 20-year average. More recent data suggest that momentum
has continued. According to Bank of America Institute, the latest monthly total credit and

debit card spending data per household rose 6.2% year-over-year (YoY) in September, and
spending excluding gasoline was up 5.1% YoY, both the third strongest growth rates in the
past four years. 1
The persistence of consumer spending is particularly notable given that temporary cyclical
boosts like larger tax refunds are starting to fade. That raises an important question: What is
actually funding strong consumption today? Recent data suggest the drivers are becoming

increasingly more diversified, with broadening labor market trends, healthy consumer
finances and elevated wealth effects all reinforcing one another and underpinning consumers.
First, the labor market continues to provide an important foundation for consumer spending,
and recent trends suggest wage growth is supporting a broader set of households than in
recent years. Jobs growth has slowed from the exceptionally strong pace seen following the
pandemic, but broader indicators suggest labor market conditions remain relatively stable.

Although the September jobs report came in softer than expected, the three- and six-month
rolling averages indicate that the trend in jobs growth likely bottomed out last year and has
gradually improved since (Exhibit 1A). Meanwhile, the unemployment rate remains low by
historical standards and layoffs remain contained. And while wage growth has moderated
and recently trailed headline inflation (Exhibit 1B), cumulative income gains over the past
several years have likely helped many households absorb higher prices better.

An important development though has been improving wage growth trends among lower-
income households, where spending tends to be most sensitive to changes in wages. Bank
of America Institute consumer deposit account data show after-tax wage growth for lower-
income households outpaced higher-income households for a second consecutive month
in August, rising 4.7% YoY compared with 3.5% for higher-income households. Importantly,
this improvement appears to be supporting discretionary spending, not just spending

on necessities. Lower-income discretionary spending rose 5.7% YoY in August, nearly
matching the 5.9% pace for higher-income households. In other words, the narrowing gap
in wage and spending growth across income cohorts suggests consumer resilience looks
to be becoming less dependent on affluent households alone, creating a broader and more
durable foundation for consumption.
Second, household finances remain in relatively good shape, providing consumers with the

flexibility to continue spending. The ratio of household debt to disposable personal income
remains near its lowest level since the late 1990s, excluding the temporary distortions
caused by pandemic-related income support in 2020 and 2021 (Exhibit 1C). Recent
revisions to the Bureau of Economic Analysis national income accounts also suggest
consumers have a larger financial buffer than previous data suggested. Personal saving
estimates over the past year have been revised higher by $320 billion, or 39%, and the

revised savings rate remains above 4% after previous estimates showed it fell to 3% during
the summer.2 Concerns that consumers may be rapidly exhausting their savings buffers
may therefore be overstated. Supporting this view, Bank of America Institute consumer
1 Bank of America Institute, “Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers.” As of October 9, 2026.
2 Bank of America, “Massive Savings Markup = Another Reason to Be Bullish.” As of October 2, 2026.

Investment Implications
Consumer spending strength
remains a key pillar of economic
and earnings growth, which keeps
us constructive on our Equity
overweight. The growing connection
between household wealth and
spending reinforces the importance
of maintaining a diversified portfolio.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
deposit account data show that middle- and lower-income households continue to maintain
deposit balances above prepandemic levels across age groups.
Third, elevated household wealth continues to provide a powerful tailwind for consumer
spending, particularly among households that account for an outsized share of overall
consumption. U.S. household net worth rose $12.8 trillion (T) to a record $195.9T in Q2,

driven largely by gains in Equities, which contributed $10.7T of the increase (Exhibit 1D).
As a result, household net worth relative to disposable income climbed to a record 8.3,
meaning households now hold more than eight dollars of wealth for every dollar of annual
disposable income. Households are also sitting on $35.8T in home equity, an all-time high
level that provides a significant balance sheet cushion. Rising asset values have helped to

support spending through what economists refer to as wealth effects. In other words, when
households feel wealthier, they tend to be more willing to spend.
For investors, the key takeaway is that consumer spending is being supported by multiple
engines despite the weaker sentiment data. Labor income trends are supporting wider
participation, healthy balance sheets are helping households absorb economic pressures,
and elevated household wealth continues to sustain higher-income spending. Together,

these forces are supportive of economic growth and corporate profits, which is particularly
important since the consumer accounts for roughly 70% of U.S. gross domestic product.
That said, it does not eliminate risk. A meaningful risk to the consumer would be a scenario
in which both the labor and wealth engines weaken simultaneously. For now, labor market
conditions remain stable, and wealth effects remain supportive. Equity market pullbacks in
recent years have done little to derail consumer spending, suggesting it would likely take a

deeper and more prolonged drawdown to alter consumer behavior.

MARKET VIEW
The Bull Market Turns Four: Older, Unloved—But Still Kicking
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
Four years ago this month, virtually no one was in the mood to buy U.S. Equities, and for
good reasons.
Rewind to October 2022: Inflation the month before clocked in at an annual rate of 8.2%.

The yield curve was inverted, a signal that has preceded most past recessions. Consumer
sentiment was abysmal, having dropped to a record low in June 2022. Recession
concerns were mounting, with an October Wall Street Journal poll survey putting the
odds of a recession within 12 months at about 63%. And realizing (belatedly) that
inflation was not “transitory,” the Fed was in the midst of administering one of the most
aggressive monetary tightening cycles in decades, raising the fed funds rate five times

between March and September 2022 by a stunning 300 basis points.
Reflecting the mood at the moment, the S&P 500 closed at 3,577 on October 12, 2022,
down 25% below its January peak, leaving investors battered and bruised but blind to the
fact that the market had bottomed. Four years on, the bull market that emerged from
the gloom of 2022 is still standing and kicking, with the S&P 500 up roughly 130% since
the October 2022 lows.
The more than doubling of the S&P over the past four years has hardly been linear—and

has been accompanied with plenty of pain and doubt. Exhibit 2 maps the rocky path
trodden by the bull over the past four years. Less than six months into the rally, the
markets were rocked by the failure of Silicon Valley Bank (SVB) on March 10, 2023. The
S&P Regional Banks Select Industry Index lost 18% that week and stoked concerns of
massive deposit runs and credit stress across the capital markets. However, owing to
emergency intervention by the government, lower yields and strength elsewhere in the

markets, the crisis passed. Some banks failed that month, but the bull market didn’t.
Exhibit 2: Revisiting the Rocky Path Trodden by the Bull.
Period "Reason to Sell" What happened instead
October 2022 Inflation >8%, aggressive Fed tightening, recession concerns Bull market begins
March 2023 SVB/regional banking crisis Stocks keep climbing
2023-24 Inverted yield curve, “recession inevitable” Economy keeps expanding
2024 “Magnificent 7* bubble,” narrow breadth, expensive valuations Earnings propel market higher

2025 Tariffs, trade war, policy uncertainty Market absorbs another shock
2026 Oil/diesel shock, 5%+ yields, Fed tightening, geopolitical risks Bull reaches fourth birthday
*Apple, Amazon, Alphabet, Nvidia, Meta, Microsoft and Tesla. Source: Chief Investment Office. As of October 9, 2026.
But recessionary concerns hung like threatening clouds over the markets for most of
2023. Aggressive Fed tightening, an inverted yield curve, and soft economic data were

all taken as harbingers of an economic slowdown and profits recession. Yet the S&P 500
continued to climb in 2023, up 26.3% for the year, driven in large part by the outsized
price performance of the Magnificent Seven.
Investors spent much of 2024 fretting about a market too dependent on technology,
and therefore a market too narrow and too expensive. The unwinding of the Japan carry
trade in August 2024 engendered more angst, as did uncertainties around the 2024

U.S. presidential election. But despite a host of misgivings, the S&P 500 delivered total
returns of 25% in 2024. The unloved bull marched on.
Total returns in 2025 were robust as well—roughly 18%—notwithstanding—yet
again—a number of headwinds. In late January, DeepSeek, a cheaper China AI model,
raised questions about America’s technology leadership and the need to spend enormous
sums on chips and data centers. The Nasdaq promptly dropped by 3.1% thereafter.

Investment Implications
The adoption phase of the AI buildout
should translate to broader earnings
growth and performance across
sectors, reinforcing our conviction
in U.S. Equities. Given a strong
fundamental backdrop, we would
continue to buy on weakness as the
bull enters its fifth year

### Page 5

5 of 8 October 13, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
January’s jolt was followed by “Liberation Day,” or the warning of punishing U.S. tariffs
on America’s major trading partners. The trade-policy shock of April 2025 caused the
S&P 500 to drop nearly 19% from its February high to its April 8 low on uncertainty
that higher import costs would decrease corporate margins and consumer spending and
upend global supply chains. Amid the trade tumult, Treasury prices fell and the U.S. dollar

weakened on concerns that foreigners were abandoning U.S. assets. Remember all the
angst over the dollar debasement trade? If that wasn’t enough, expensive valuations and
enormous capital commitments raised doubts about the AI trade and triggered talk of an
AI bubble. Yet despite this wall of worry, the S&P 500, for the third year running, posted
double-digit gains in 2025.
And double-digit gains could be in store this year, with the S&P 500 up 14% through

October 9. The bull has rumbled on, taking in stride the energy shock from the war in
the Middle East, the shift in interest rate expectations from the Fed (from rate cuts
to rate hikes), and the rising uncertainty associated with the midterm elections. These
headwinds have been countered by tailwinds such as better-than-expected corporate
earnings and better-than-anticipated real growth.
Finally, as Exhibit 3 makes clear, what has given this bull so much muscle has been

the U.S. Technology sector, specifically the Magnificent 7. Powering the bull has been
the Magnificent 7, with a staggering 344% return since the 2022 lows, while U.S.
technology has delivered a 277% return versus the S&P 500’s return of 130%. Note the
outperformance of the S&P 500 relative to the main international indices.
Looking forward: Is the bull’s greatest strength also its Achilles’ heel? Bull
markets don’t die of natural causes. And they typically don’t expire because of one

particular signal or indicator. Bull markets turn to bear markets when a cluster of signals/
metrics turn south. Think deteriorating and downward earnings revisions, higher interest
rates, widening credit spreads and tighter lending standards, growth disappointments,
stretched valuations and sentiment, deteriorating market breadth, and an external
shock. Any one of these signals could flash red and not trouble the bull.  But when they
converge or feed off each other, that’s trouble.

In addition, since a great deal of the earnings growth, market leadership and capital
expenditures (capex) spending of this bull market have come from the technology sector,
the bull’s greatest strength could become its greatest vulnerability. AI has become an
investment ecosystem unto itself, and hence the fifth year of the bull market will pivot
in part around AI revenue, frontier model adoption rates, infrastructure spending, free
cash flows, depreciation costs, debt issuance, data center utilization, cloud growth, and

competition via cheaper models and computing alternatives. To put it mildly, there is a
great deal at stake when it comes to the bull and AI.
However, this is where we stand today: We believe the bull will continue to rumble
ahead next year, with AI revenues and productivity gains justifying the costs of the AI
infrastructure. Critically, AI is moving from the buildout phase, dominated by chipmakers
and hyperscaler capex, into the adoption phase, where software firms, healthcare,

finance, energy and a myriad of other firms start to see productivity gains and margin
expansion from rising AI adoption rates. We believe this will give better breadth to the
S&P 500; so too will continued profit growth across various U.S. sectors, AI investment
that starts to pay off, and an economy capable of absorbing setbacks or periodic bouts
of uncertainty.
The path forward won’t be linear—and there will be fits and starts, and market
drawdowns. That’s normal for any market. But that aside, the bull will keep rumbling,

roaming and kicking in 2027.
